| S-70 | [BLOCKING] Containment and its proof | awaiting-verification | 6643d39 · verify-round-1 8097300 | 2026-09-09 | **Written entirely off-machine; the proof (V-01..V-04, phios-agente.md §15) is BLOCKING and has not run.** No write capability anywhere in M7 is real until the user pastes all four results. New `profiles/desktop/home/.local/bin/phi-agent-contain` (bash + coreutils + bubblewrap only): the single launch path for both opencode instances — the S-71/S-74 units and `phi agent ask` all `exec` through it, and there is no code path that starts an agent outside `bwrap` (§4.7, ADR 090). Containment is built FROM EMPTY (§4.1, ADR 087): a tmpfs root, `--proc`/`--dev`/`--tmpfs /tmp`/`--tmpfs /run`, then only the binds the two `mounts/*.paths` files name. Namespaces exactly as §4.6: `--unshare-pid --unshare-ipc --unshare-uts --unshare-cgroup --new-session --die-with-parent`, plus `--unshare-user-try` (not a perimeter decision — it is the mechanism that lets the other unshares be created unprivileged on a non-setuid bwrap, which is the Arch package's default; documented in the script). `--clearenv` then an explicit `--setenv` allowlist (HOME, PATH, TERM, LANG, the four XDG_*_HOME pointing at the container's own trees, XDG_RUNTIME_DIR, OPENCODE_CONFIG, NO_PROXY, PHI_AGENT_INSTANCE, and for A2 the GIT_AUTHOR/COMMITTER identity) — `SSH_AUTH_SOCK`, `DBUS_SESSION_BUS_ADDRESS`, `WAYLAND_DISPLAY`, and every `*_API_KEY`/`*_TOKEN` are excluded by construction, not by a filter (§4.6). Fail-closed preflight (V-04): the script exits non-zero, printing one line and running nothing, if `bwrap` is absent / non-executable / `--version` fails, or if `kernel.unprivileged_userns_clone`/`user.max_user_namespaces` is 0 on a non-setuid bwrap, or if any required (unsuffixed) mount source is missing — so a unit with this as `ExecStart` lands in `failed` with no engine process. `mounts/common.paths` = §4.2 base (the `/usr` tree + `/bin`,`/sbin`,`/lib`,`/lib64` symlinks, `resolv.conf`/`hosts`/`nsswitch.conf`, the CA bundle, `passwd`/`group`, `localtime`), all read-only. `mounts/a1.paths` = §4.3: A1's own XDG trees writable, `opencode.json` read-only (single resolved file, not the `.config` dir — it is a dotfiles symlink), the active project bound read-only first with `proposte/` and `output/` re-bound writable underneath (so `memoria.md`/`materiali/`/`archivio/` are read-only with no explicit rule — S-73 makes that explicit), the note archive read-only. `mounts/a2.paths` = §4.4: code root + A2 XDG trees + A2-only toolchain cache writable, nothing else; git identity via env, never a mounted `.gitconfig`; A2 also gets `--unshare-net` from the script, so it has NO network until S-72 adds the bridge — the safe direction, and V-03's "denied" holds trivially in the meantime. `mounts/never.paths` is not read by the launcher: it is the machine-readable V-01/V-02 checklist (SSH keys, GPG, password store, mail, browser profiles, `/var` `/srv` `/opt` `/boot` `/efi`, `/etc` beyond the whitelist, and the session sockets). Off-machine self-test: `bash -n` clean; argument assembly exercised with a stub `bwrap` (`--dry-run`) for both instances — the emitted argv matches §4.6 for namespaces, the allowlist contains no secret name, A2 carries `--unshare-net` and A1 does not, `ro`/`rw` ordering puts the writable project subdirs after the read-only project bind. `env.example` (paths + git identity only, explicitly no secret) and a `README.md` for `~/.config/phi-agent/`. `bubblewrap` + `opencode` added to `profiles/desktop/packages.txt` (master plan §15.3, both hosts carry `desktop`). **Cannot be verified here at all — no Linux host, no bubblewrap on the build machine.** V-01 (secrets unreadable from inside), V-02 (env free of secrets and the SSH-agent socket), V-03 (A2 egress denied with and without proxy vars), V-04 (containment made unavailable → startup fails) are all USER operations on `zotac` and `razer`; see the end-of-M7 verification list. Held: exact behaviour of `--unshare-cgroup` on the real kernels, whether opencode honours all four `XDG_*_HOME` for full instance separation, and the real note-archive path (left as `PHI_AGENT_NOTES_DIR`, default `~/notes`). **Verification round 1 (razer, 2 bugs, fixed same step — §16.1):** (1) `phi-agent-contain` was shipped in `~/.local/bin/` but nothing put that dir on `PATH`, so the bare name (used interactively and by `phi-code`) was not found; the systemd units were unaffected (they call `%h/.local/bin/...`). Fixed: `profiles/base/home/.zshenv` prepends `~/.local/bin` (`typeset -U`). (2) `env.example` shipped `GIT_AUTHOR_NAME=phi agent` — the file is *sourced*, so the unquoted space ran `agent` as a command and, under `set -e`, aborted the launcher with a bare "command not found". Fixed: quote it, reword the header comment to say the file is sourced and spaces need quoting, and `phi-agent-contain` now pre-scans the env file and refuses with one clear line naming the offending `KEY=a b`. After both fixes, **V-01, V-02, V-04 (script half) all passed on razer** (a1 + a2: every secret path "not found"; env is the allowlist only, no `SSH_AUTH_SOCK`/`*_TOKEN`; bwrap-gone → `containment unavailable`, exit 1, no process). Dry-run argv inspected for both instances: built from empty, no bind near real `$HOME`, a2 has `--unshare-net` + the socket bridge, a1 does not. Still pending: V-03 (needs S-72 services) and V-04 unit half (needs `phi-agent-a1.service`). Status stays `awaiting-verification` until Gate G7. |
| S-71 | Credential brokering | awaiting-verification | phi 4cdbbe5 · phi-packages 422e367 · dotfiles ccc660c · verify-round-1 8097300 bf3eb89 | 2026-09-09 | Depends on S-70 (itself unverified — the boundary this broker assumes has not been proven; see S-70). `phi agent broker` (new `phi agent` verb group, `internal/agent/`): a loopback `httputil.ReverseProxy` with `FlushInterval = -1` so every upstream chunk is flushed to opencode immediately — the buffer-free streaming §6.2 requires and V-09 tests. The client's own `authorization`/`x-api-key` headers are stripped in `Rewrite` and the real key attached from a file; `extra_headers` and a configurable auth header/value template keep it provider-agnostic (no planning doc names the provider, §2.1). Key resolution order: `PHI_AGENT_BROKER_KEY_FILE` (set by the unit from `LoadCredential`), then `$CREDENTIALS_DIRECTORY/provider-key`, then `<config>/phi-agent/<inst>/provider-key` for a hand-run broker — the key is a literal nowhere and in no repository (ADR 092). `listen` is validated to be a loopback address. Fail-closed: missing config or unreadable/empty key → the process exits non-zero and never binds, so the unit stays `failed` and, since opencode's only route to the provider is this port, no agent works (§6.2, consistent with §4.7). `ratelimit.go` is a fixed-window request cap (complement to the provider spending cap, §6.3/§6.4); `meter.go` appends one JSONL record per request (`time`, `instance`, `method`, `path`, `status`, `model`, `resp_bytes`, `dur_ms`) to `~/.local/state/phi-agent/<inst>/broker-meter.jsonl` — token-level accounting is **deferred** until a provider is chosen, because parsing the `usage` block out of a streaming response body is provider-specific and awkward; request/byte/model metering is real now. **Verified locally (not on hardware):** `internal/agent/broker_test.go` — a delayed-chunk mock upstream proves chunks arrive with their inter-chunk delay preserved (not bunched at the end); the client's placeholder auth is dropped and the real key reaches upstream; the rate limit returns 429 and does not forward; the meter writes a parseable record; `requireLoopback` rejects `0.0.0.0` and routable hosts. An end-to-end `curl` through a running `phi agent broker` against a local mock confirmed the same streaming timing and `--check`. Dotfiles: both `opencode.json` files gain a `provider.phi-broker` block (`@ai-sdk/openai-compatible`, `baseURL` → `127.0.0.1:8789`/`:8790`, dummy `apiKey`) and `model` — the model id and, for an Anthropic-native provider, `npm`/`baseURL`/auth header are the user's to set (two clearly-marked `REPLACE-WITH-YOUR-MODEL-ID` spots + README). `a1/broker.example.json` is the broker-config template. `phi-agent-broker@.service` (templated user unit): `ExecStartPre` runs `--check`, `LoadCredential` pulls the key from `%h/.config/phi-agent/%i/provider-key`, plus a §11 hardening block (resource caps, `ProtectSystem=strict`, `SystemCallFilter=@system-service`, etc. — **written from `systemd.exec(5)`, unrun**; loosen `MemoryDenyWriteExecute`/`SystemCallFilter` first if it fails to start). Declared in `services-user.txt`, never enabled. **USER, before enabling anything:** set a hard spending cap on the provider key and write the revocation procedure down off-machine (§6.3 — the cap limits damage, not probability). **USER verifications:** V-08 (no provider key anywhere inside the containment) and V-09 (streaming through the broker is incremental) — see the end-of-M7 list. Held: which provider, and therefore the real `upstream`/`auth_header`/model; whether opencode fetches the `@ai-sdk/*` npm package at runtime (would need npm on A2's whitelist / a first online run for A1). **M7 review pass (phi `1e3550e`):** `TestBrokerMeterRecords` was racy (~1 in 4 failing in isolation — the meter line is written by the handler goroutine after `ServeHTTP` returns, which the client seeing EOF does not order against); now polls for the line. Broker code unchanged. **Verification round 1 (razer):** `a{1,2}/opencode/opencode.json` shipped as repo symlinks carrying a `REPLACE-WITH-YOUR-MODEL-ID` placeholder — editing them to set the model id dirtied the dotfiles working tree and would fight `git pull`, the exact problem `broker.example.json` avoids. Renamed to `opencode.example.json` (git mv); the user copies to `opencode.json` (real file, theirs). `mounts/a{1,2}.paths` opencode.json bind changed `ro` → `ro?` so V-01..V-04 still run on a machine with zero provider setup (a bare `sh` needs no engine config). README + verify procedures updated. Broker/CLI unchanged. **Verified on razer:** the broker's own machinery — `--check` loads the key and prints the summary; a request to `127.0.0.1:8789` is forwarded to the configured `upstream` with the placeholder auth replaced by the real key (the meter recorded `POST /v1/chat/completions` with the upstream's status and `dur_ms`); **V-08 passed** — `find / -name provider-key` inside the containment returns nothing, no key-shaped env var, and `opencode.json` inside carries only the dummy `apiKey`. **V-09 NOT verified.** First provider tried was opencode Zen (`https://opencode.ai/zen`): the key lists models fine but `/v1/chat/completions` returns 401 — Zen requires billing to be enabled, which the user is not doing. Switching to **OpenRouter** (`upstream=https://openrouter.ai/api`, prepaid balance = the §6.3 cap, model tentatively `deepseek/deepseek-chat`); the raw-curl streaming test (V-09) and everything downstream is **on hold pending the user's OpenRouter key**. The broker correctly surfaced the upstream 401 rather than masking it, which is the intended behaviour, but incremental streaming through it is still unproven against a real provider. |
| S-72 | A2 network whitelist | awaiting-verification | phi 44f1042 · phi-packages 2544f69 · dotfiles 2892363 | 2026-09-09 | Depends on S-70 (unverified). The boundary is the namespace, not a variable (§5.1): `phi-agent-contain` already gives A2 `--unshare-net` (S-70); this step adds the only routes out. Two unix sockets in `~/.local/state/phi-agent/net/`, bind-mounted into the container as `/run/phi-agent/net/`: `broker-a2.sock` (the provider call, created by `phi-agent-broker@a2` which now listens on `unix:` instead of TCP) and `proxy.sock` (everything else, created by `phi-agent-net-bridge.service` = `socat UNIX-LISTEN … TCP:127.0.0.1:8888` → `tinyproxy`). Inside the namespace `phi-agent-contain` wraps the command with two `socat` forwarders turning those sockets into `127.0.0.1:8790` and `127.0.0.1:8118`, and sets `HTTP(S)_PROXY` to the latter with `NO_PROXY=127.0.0.1,localhost` so the broker call goes straight to its own forwarder and every other host goes through the proxy. **V-03 discriminator:** a process that unsets the proxy vars is left able to reach *only* the broker (which forwards solely to the configured provider) — never a free network, because the namespace has no route and the proxy socket is the sole external channel. `tinyproxy/tinyproxy.conf` (`FilterDefaultDeny Yes`, loopback listen, `Allow 127.0.0.1`; `__HOME__`/`__RUNTIME__` rendered by the unit's `ExecStartPre` sed since tinyproxy has no `~`/env expansion) + `tinyproxy/whitelist` — **ships with loopback allowed and every package registry commented out**; the user uncomments exactly what a project on the machine fetches from (§5.1 "si estende per aggiunta esplicita, mai per comodità"). The broker is deliberately NOT a whitelist line — A2 reaches it on its dedicated socket, not through tinyproxy. `socat` + `tinyproxy` added to `profiles/desktop/packages.txt` (§15.3). `phi-agent-proxy.service` + `phi-agent-net-bridge.service` declared in `services-user.txt`, never enabled; both only needed once A2 runs (S-76). **Deviation, flagged:** §5.1 describes one unix bridge to a proxy whose whitelist "includes the brokering service address"; here the broker gets its own dedicated socket instead of a tinyproxy allow-rule, because (a) opencode/undici bypasses the proxy for loopback destinations regardless of `NO_PROXY`, making a `127.0.0.1` allow-rule unreliable, and (b) separating "our trusted service" from "filtered external egress" is cleaner. Same end state: A2 reaches the provider only through the broker, and arbitrary hosts only through the filtered proxy. **Unverified end to end** — no Linux host, no bwrap, no socat chain exercised. `phi`'s unix-socket listen is unit-tested (`TestBrokerUnixListen`, `TestRequireLocalListen`). **USER verification:** V-03 (A2 egress to a non-whitelisted domain denied with AND without proxy vars) — see the end-of-M7 list. Held: whether opencode fetches `@ai-sdk/*` from npm at first run (would need `registry.npmjs.org` uncommented in the whitelist); the exact `socat` address syntax on the real machine; whether `tinyproxy` in `extra` is built with filtering support. |

Names validated to `^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9._-]{0,63}$` — `../evil`, `a/b`, `.hidden` rejected (test-covered). **memoria.md write path:** exactly one — `phi agent memory accept FILE` appends the **literal** proposal text (shown first by `memory show` as a `+`-prefixed diff against the current file, never a summary — §8.6) and deletes the proposal; `reject` just deletes. The agent never writes memory; the read-only mount (`mounts/a1.paths`, made explicit this step) enforces it, not the interface (§8.4, ADR 094). **phi MCP server** (`phi agent mcp`, `internal/agent/mcp.go`): newline-delimited JSON-RPC 2.0 on stdio, **exactly one** read-only no-argument tool `phi_context` (active project + `progetto.md` + `memoria.md` + personality list). `mcpTools` is the single growth point (§7.1). Works inside the containment (fixed `/home/agent` paths) and outside it. **dotfiles side:** `phi-agent-contain` now reads the `active-project` marker as a fallback to `$PHI_AGENT_PROJECT`. `phi-agent-a1.service` = `phi-agent-contain a1 -- opencode serve --hostname 127.0.0.1 --port 4199`, `Requires=phi-agent-broker@a1`, no fallback ExecStart (so it stays `failed` if bwrap is gone — V-04). `a1/opencode/opencode.json` gains: `mcp.phi` (`command: ["phi","agent","mcp"]`), an `agent` block mapping `general`/`technical` to `{file:/home/agent/.local/share/personalita/*.md}` with `default_agent: general`, and `instructions: [progetto.md, memoria.md]` (memoria always in context, §8.5). Declared `phi-agent-a1.service` in `services-user.txt`, never enabled. **Verified locally:** `go test ./internal/agent/` (MCP handshake, seed idempotency, project lifecycle, name rejection, literal-append promotion); manual `phi agent init`/`project`/`memory`/`mcp` end-to-end against a temp HOME. **USER verifications:** V-05 (write to `memoria.md` from inside the containment → denied), V-06 (write outside `output/`+`proposte/` → denied), V-07 (agent reads its own XDG tree → denied), V-10 (prompt carries personality + project instructions). Held: whether opencode's `agent.*.prompt` `{file:...}` errors when the personality file is briefly absent; whether `instructions` tolerates a missing `progetto.md` when no project is selected. **M7 review pass (phi `1e3550e`, phi-shell `9dd04d2`):** `phi agent memory list` was emitting its decorated form unconditionally, and the shell panel filtered lines by shape (no colon, no space) — a proposal file the agent named with a colon or a space would silently never appear in the pending list, the one failure §8.6 cannot tolerate. `list` now prints one bare file name per line when redirected (the output contract), and the panel takes every non-empty line. `ProposalText`/`AcceptProposal`/`RejectProposal` now reject a `name` that is not a single path segment (was `filepath.Join`-ed straight from a CLI arg / the panel). New tests: `TestModelProposalNameTraversal`, `view.TestAgentMemoryList*`. `phi-packages` bumped to 0.12.1 (`7e23ef7`) tracking phi `1e3550e` — the panel and `phi agent memory list` changed together, so the packaged `phi` must be rebuilt at ≥ `1e3550e` before the S-75 checks. **Verification round 1 (razer):** **V-05 passed** — `echo >> /home/agent/project/memoria.md` from inside the containment fails "Read-only file system". **V-06 passed** — of `output/x proposte/x materiali/x <project root>/y notes/x personalita/x`, only `output/` and `proposte/` accept a write; the rest are denied. `phi agent init` / `project new` / `project use --no-restart` all worked against the real `~/.local/share/phi-agent/a1` tree. **V-07 and V-10 still unverified** — both need opencode actually running, which is blocked on the provider (see S-71). MCP `phi_context` not yet exercised on hardware. |

Password: `LoadCredential` from `~/.config/phi-agent/a2/remote-password` (user, mode 600, out of repo); `phi-agent-contain --remote` **bind-mounts** the credential file and the wrapper reads it into `OPENCODE_SERVER_PASSWORD` *inside* the container — never a `--setenv` value, which would expose it in `bwrap`'s argv (verified in the dry-run: only `OPENCODE_SERVER_USERNAME` reaches setenv). `--remote` is A2-only and errors on `a1`. `env.example` gains a commented `PHI_AGENT_REMOTE_ADDR`. `a1/opencode/opencode.json` `external_directory` changed from bare `"deny"` to an object (allow `/home/agent/notes/**` and `.../personalita/**`, deny opencode's own XDG trees) so J3 and V-07 both hold whichever way opencode resolves the workspace root — advisor item 1, a fix to the S-73 file, made here. **Stray:** this step's `phi` commit also carries a one-line gofmt alignment fix in `internal/theme/set.go` (pre-existing dirt, not S-74 work). **Verified locally:** `go test ./internal/agent/` (ask), dry-run of `phi-agent-contain a2 --remote` (password bind not setenv; a1 rejected; missing-credential dies). **USER verifications:** V-15 (inline session absent from the panel list), V-17 (remote surface listening only on the overlay interface), V-18 (remote access with no credential → denied). Held: the whole inbound socat chain and opencode server-auth behaviour are unrun; whether opencode's session-delete also purges its title-generation/summary side effects; the real overlay address and whether `socat`'s `bind=` accepts it as written. |

| S-76 | Transition from the current uncontained usage | awaiting-verification | dotfiles e8ab747 + review-pass f1937c1 + this commit · phi 1e3550e · phi-shell 9dd04d2 · phi-packages 7e23ef7 | 2026-09-09 | Depends on S-75 (unverified). The card has no `AGENT` bullets — it is a `USER` transition, gated on V-01..V-04 and V-08/V-09 all passing first. Agent deliverable is the mechanism to make the switch ergonomic + the runbook: `profiles/desktop/home/.config/zsh/agent.zsh` (new) defines `phi-code [DIR]` — `phi-agent-contain a2 -- opencode` (the TUI in the container) for `DIR` under `PHI_AGENT_CODE_ROOT`, with a guard that refuses a path outside it; plus `alias phi-ask='phi agent ask'`. `.zshrc` (base) gains one line that sources `$ZDOTDIR/agent.zsh` **only if present** — the file ships in the `desktop` profile, so `mini` (base only) never gets it. `phi-agent-a2.service`'s header reworked: it is now documented as an OPTIONAL persistent server (its port is inside A2's netns; reaching it from the host needs a user-added bridge), and daily interactive use is `phi-code`. README gains a "Transition" section: enable the A2 support services, configure `a2/broker.json`+`provider-key`+model+whitelist, run a real task with `phi-code` and confirm from inside that `cat ~/.ssh/id_*` and `git push` fail while `git commit` works, then `mv ~/.config/opencode ~/.config/opencode.pre-phios.bak` once satisfied. **`DONE WHEN`: the old unconfined configuration is removed** — a USER action, not something the agent can do. **VERIFY: a real coding session completes under containment** — USER, `phi-code` in a real project, real work, finished. **Gate G7** (`docs/phios-agent-brief.md`): all of `V-01`…`V-18` executed and recorded — NONE have been; every M7 row is `awaiting-verification` and the whole milestone was written against a containment boundary that has never run on a real machine. **M7 review pass (this commit):** `phi-code`'s in-container `cd` to `/home/agent/code/<rel>` was `cd … || cd /home/agent/code` — on any translation failure it silently opened the *root* of every project, the §4.3 perimeter inverted. Now fatal: a bad `<rel>` prints an error and exits, never a fallback. The host-side prefix test and `rel` computation both `:A`-resolve `code_root` (symlinks and `..` gone) so they agree, and the prefix is now quoted so a code root containing a glob char is matched literally; a project dir whose real path escapes the code root (reached via a symlink) is refused — correct, since that path is not under the bind. Held: the actual daily-use ergonomics (the card's own test is "use it for a week"). |

The shell's selection grammar uses its own `colorMain`/`colorOpposite` roles, **not** `PHI_SELECTION_BG`/`FG` (those stay carried from kitty and feed the yazi/btop/nvim templates untouched). | OOP-12 | Shell restyle R2 — runner | verified | phi-shell 4f99c01 · dotfiles n/a | 2026-09-09 | Batch 2 of `shell-restyle-r2`, phi-shell only (`Launcher/Launcher.qml`). **Centred, not top-anchored** (user: "the runner should be centred") — `panelWrap` now `verticalCenter` with a small upward bias. **Fixed tall height from the moment it opens** (user: "it should start at the highest height, eg. 20 entries"): the result list is a fixed-height `Flickable` (`rowH * 20`, capped at 62% of screen height) inside the box, so the panel does not grow and shrink as results change; >20 results scroll, the highlighted row is kept in view. **Applications shown by default** (user: "show the applications list by default"): with nothing typed, `browseResults` is built from `Quickshell.DesktopEntries.applications` (the same freedesktop `.desktop` source phi's own `ApplicationsProvider` scans; `phi query ""` returns nothing by design and the packaged binary is frozen for M7, so the browse list is shell-side), sorted by name; a new `desktopEntry` action kind runs `DesktopEntry.execute()` (falls back to its cleaned exec string). A typed query still goes to `phi query` for ranking. Keyboard nav + Enter/Tab now read a `displayResults` that is the browse list when empty, the ranked list otherwise. **Coherent sizes** (user: "font sizes are not coherent between input and options, path should be smaller"): input, prefix and the option name are all `sizeStep 2`; the directory/subtitle drops to `sizeStep 0` and stays italic + muted. **Wider** (user): `max(48ch, screen·0.34)`, was `max(34ch, 0.25)`. **Φ prefix** gets more air (`"Φ   :   "`). **Fast highlight**: the name-only selection block fades in/out over 70 ms (`Behavior on opacity`) instead of snapping. `DesktopEntries.applications` is guarded (`… && .values || []`) — worst case the browse list is empty and typed queries still work; flagged. New/changed: `Launcher/Launcher.qml`. Not verified — no compositor here. |
| OOP-13 | Shell restyle R2 — cheatsheet spacing, settings search | verified | phi-shell bb702fe · dotfiles a758e99 | 2026-09-09 | Batch 2 of `shell-restyle-r2`, phi-shell only. **Cheatsheet** (user: "spacing should be better"): row spacing `space1`→`space2`, inter-column gap `space2`→`space3`, and the key column is now the longest visible combination + one cell of padding, **capped at 35 cells** so a single long binding cannot shove the description column off to the right (`Cheatsheet/Cheatsheet.qml`). The per-character spacing was already removed in OOP-09. **Settings search into section contents** (user: "searchbar should look into the sections as well, not just the section title"): each `Settings/sections.json` entry gains a `keywords` string (the settings that section contains), and `sectionMatches()` checks title OR keywords; when the filter hides the active section the content pane jumps to the first section that still matches. A true per-setting-row index is a larger job (every section renders its own rows independently) — this keyword list is an approximation, flagged. The oversized close button was fixed in OOP-09. New/changed: `Cheatsheet/Cheatsheet.qml`, `Settings/Settings.qml`, `Settings/sections.json`. Not verified — no compositor here. |
| OOP-14 | Shell restyle R2 — themed-software coherence (partial) | verified | dotfiles this commit | 2026-09-09 | Batch 2 of `shell-restyle-r2`, dotfiles only, addressing the user's "yazi, neovim, librewolf, zsh and all other themed softwares are not styled coherently". **What this does:** (1) the Neovim overlay (`profiles/base/templates/.config/nvim/lua/theme.lua.tmpl`) went from 7 highlight groups to the full set of stable, always-present built-in groups — structure, statusline/tabline/Pmenu, messages, the Tier-3 syntax groups, diff, spell and `vim.diagnostic.*` — so an untouched base colorscheme is pulled onto the phi palette instead of only `Normal`/`Comment`/`Search`. Plugin and Tree-sitter groups are still a later pass (they vary by setup). (2) the Italian "Generato da theme.sh — non modificare a mano" header (CLAUDE.md English-only) fixed in the zsh and kitty theme templates. **What this does NOT do, and why** — flagged for a dedicated verified pass on real hardware: yazi's `theme.toml` is only partially covered (file list + git + markers; `[status]`/`[mode]`/`[pick]`/`[input]`/etc. absent) and yazi rejects an unknown key strictly enough to fall back to its default theme, so expanding it blind is a regression risk; kitty / yazi / btop / nvim all still have `[unknown]` in `design/adapters.txt`'s reload column, so a running instance keeps its old colours until restarted (`phi theme set` renders the file, nothing pushes it in — wiring `kitty @ set-colors` needs `allow_remote_control`, a security-posture change, not a theme fix); Librewolf's `userChrome.css` has a template but no `adapters.txt` row at all (the profile dir has a random prefix — see that template's own header), so `phi theme set` never renders it. New/changed: `profiles/base/templates/.config/nvim/lua/theme.lua.tmpl`, `profiles/base/templates/.config/zsh/theme.zsh.tmpl`, `profiles/desktop/templates/.config/kitty/theme.conf.tmpl`. Apply: `git pull` dotfiles → `phi theme set <variant>` → restart the affected apps. Not verified — no way to render these here. |

| OOP-16 | Shell restyle R3 — B: modal scrim covers the bar | verified | phi-shell 93f25dd · dotfiles 7f50467 | 2026-09-10 | Commit B of `shell-restyle-r3`. **#1**: the settings / chat scrim did not dim the status bar, and the notification panel had no scrim at all. Cause: the modal panel windows ran `exclusiveZone: 0`, so the compositor shrank them out of the bar's reserved strip and they also sat on the same layer as the bar. Fix (same guard form as `Background/Background.qml`): `Settings`, `Panels/AgentPanel`, `Panels/Sidebar`, `Cheatsheet` and `Launcher` now set `exclusiveZone: -1` and `WlrLayershell.layer = WlrLayer.Overlay` in `Component.onCompleted` (merged with the existing `_animReady` handler where there was one), so their full-screen scrim covers the whole output including the bar. `Panels/Sidebar.qml` gains a `Widgets.Scrim` it never had. `Launcher` is raised too (so a click on the bar strip dismisses it) but keeps NO scrim — it stays a light overlay. `Panels/Calendar` and `Panels/BarPopout` deliberately stay on `Top` with no scrim (corner popouts; R3 #9 asks for no shadow on those). All five still gate `visible` on `shown || fadeRoot.opacity > 0`, so the Overlay surface unmaps when closed and never holds input globally. New/changed: `Settings/Settings.qml`, `Cheatsheet/Cheatsheet.qml`, `Launcher/Launcher.qml`, `Panels/AgentPanel.qml`, `Panels/Sidebar.qml`. Not verified — no compositor here. |

Volume commits live (`AudioBridge.setVolume`); brightness commits on release only (it spawns `brightnessctl`). **wifi / bluetooth / network / battery popouts** carry a compact readout (SSID / adapter+device / overlay name / charge+time-left) and a deep-link button (`nmtui`, `bluetuith`) where a TUI exists; GPU stays a pointer to the bar readout (its live values live in the module, not a service). | OOP-18 | Shell restyle R3 — D: centre-isle height / bar vertical padding | verified | phi-shell f114eea · dotfiles n/a | 2026-09-10 | Follow-up in `shell-restyle-r3`, phi-shell only. User: the active-window title isle was taller than the bar and the bar resized when an app opened/closed; the title element should be the same height as every other bar element, with only horizontal padding. Cause: OOP-15/#8 gave `centerIsle` `pad: space2`, which BarIsle applied to BOTH axes, and `Bar.qml` height keyed off `centerIsle.implicitHeight`. Fix: `Widgets/BarIsle.qml` splits `padH` (horizontal, defaults to `pad`) from `pad` (vertical); `Bar/Bar.qml` centre isle is `height: max(side isle heights)` with `pad: 0` + `padH: space2`, and the bar height `Math.max` no longer includes the centre isle — so the bar is stable regardless of the active window. `Widgets/Segment.qml` isle Segments also drop to half a rhythm unit of vertical inset (`paddingV · 0.5` on `ambient:"isle"`), for a tighter bar overall. New/changed: `Widgets/BarIsle.qml`, `Bar/Bar.qml`, `Widgets/Segment.qml`. Not verified — no compositor here. |
| OOP-19 | Shell restyle R4 — A: selected-row/panel text inversion + runner name/dir split | verified | phi-shell abdf4e9 · dotfiles this commit | 2026-09-10 | **`Out-of-plan: shell-restyle-r4`** — a fourth feedback round on the shell restyle (10 items), split by verifiability into batches A–G (rows OOP-19…OOP-25). `phi-shell` + `phios-dotfiles` only; `phi`/`phi-packages` stay frozen where M7 left them. Verification held to one screenshot pass, same as R1–R3. This is batch A, phi-shell only. **Item 9 — selection effects don't change the selected item's text colour (overlays, clipboard, settings), "works perfectly in the runner".** Root cause: `Widgets/WidgetStates.js` `surfaceColors()` "active" case inverts `bg`→contrast, but only `Widgets/Segment` (OOP-03) and `Widgets/StyledButton` ever applied the matching `fg` to their own label/icon. `Widgets/ListRow` painted its `Rectangle` from `stateColors.bg` and left `labelText`/`valueText`/`leading` on their own `contentColor()` — so a selected (inverted) nav row, agent-project row or memory-notice pick rendered fg-0 text on an fg-0 block. Fix: `ListRow` gains `labelColor` (tracks `stateColors.fg` in every state — identical to the old value except when inverted/invalid) and `valueColor` (keeps the §8.6 muted-value affordance at rest, follows the inversion only when `resolvedState === "active"`); `leading`/`labelText`/`valueText` bind to them. `Widgets/Panel` exposes `contentColor` (`invalid ? error : stateColors.fg`) for the case a Panel's content slot is a bare `StyledText` that QML cannot recolour from the parent — `Panels/tabs/Clipboard.qml` (card preview / timestamp / pin glyph) and `Panels/tabs/ChatBubble.qml` now bind to it instead of a hand-rolled `selected ? selectionText : textPrimary` ternary (same result, one source; the "you" bubble was still on `accentText`, stale since OOP-02 moved the active fill off accent). The runner's own name-only highlight (`Launcher/Launcher.qml`, OOP-05) is untouched — it already did this explicitly, which is why it worked. **Item 7 — runner: the two option texts (name, directory) should have the space between them maxed out.** `dirText` was anchored to the right edge but its box started 2ch after the name and the text was left-aligned, so it clustered near the name. Now anchored `left: nameText.right` (+ `space3`) → `right: parent.right`, `horizontalAlignment: AlignRight`, still `elide: ElideLeft` — the path hugs the right edge, tail visible, and the gap is the whole remaining row width. A pathologically long name squeezes `dirText` to nothing (acceptable, pre-existing shape). **Not verified — no compositor here.** New/changed: `Widgets/ListRow.qml`, `Widgets/Panel.qml`, `Panels/tabs/Clipboard.qml`, `Panels/tabs/ChatBubble.qml`, `Launcher/Launcher.qml`. Clipboard's card already carried an explicit equivalent of the fix, so if the user saw a concrete defect there specifically it is not reproduced from the code — flagged for the screenshot pass. |
| OOP-20 | Shell restyle R4 — B: one source for bar height, docks clear the bar | verified | phi-shell b0bfc4d · dotfiles this commit | 2026-09-10 | Batch B of `shell-restyle-r4` (see OOP-19), phi-shell only. **Item 2 — chat and notification panels cover the status bar; they should stay in the windows area while the shadow still covers the bar.** After OOP-16 the notification (`Panels/Sidebar.qml`) and chat (`Panels/AgentPanel.qml`) docks are full-screen `WlrLayer.Overlay` + `exclusiveZone: -1` so their scrim dims the bar — but the dock BODY also extended up over the bar. Fix: the dock `Item` gains `anchors.topMargin: Services.BarMetrics.height`, so the panel chrome starts below the bar; the scrim is untouched and still spans the whole output. **New `Services/BarMetrics.qml`** — a singleton holding the bar's real on-screen height. Three files each kept their own guess before this (`Panels/BarPopout.qml`: `fontSize1 + space1·ch·2`; `Panels/Calendar.qml`: `fontSize1 + space2·ch·2`; `Bar/Bar.qml`'s real `max(fontSize1, isle footprints) + islandMargin`, last changed OOP-18 — the other two already lagged it). `Bar/Bar.qml` is the only file that can measure the isle footprints, so it `report()`s its height on `onHeightChanged` + `Component.onCompleted`; every bar instance computes the same value (font metrics + tokens, never per-monitor content) so last-writer-wins is correct, and a `fallback` (`fontSize2·2.5`) covers the frame before the first report. `Panels/Calendar.qml` and `Panels/BarPopout.qml` drop their local estimates for `Services.BarMetrics.height` — this also lifts the bar popouts, which item 4 reports as sitting too low (the right-edge alignment half of item 4 is batch D). **Not verified — no compositor here**; whether `BarMetrics.report()` fires early enough that a dock never flashes at `y=0` on first open is the screenshot-pass check (the `fallback` is sized generously against exactly that). New/changed: `Services/BarMetrics.qml` (new), `Bar/Bar.qml`, `Panels/Sidebar.qml`, `Panels/AgentPanel.qml`, `Panels/Calendar.qml`, `Panels/BarPopout.qml`. |
| OOP-21 | Shell restyle R4 — C: bar buttons on the wallpaper, colours match a panel | verified | phi-shell 94b0382 · dotfiles this commit | 2026-09-10 | Batch C of `shell-restyle-r4` (see OOP-19), phi-shell only. This is R3 #7 ("remove the status bar main background"), which was HELD for clarification — the user has now cleared it and added item 6. **Item 10 — remove the background of the isles.** `Widgets/BarIsle.qml` no longer paints its `Config.Appearance.barIsleBackground` block; it is a pure layout container (kept as a type for the three-island layout + the centre isle's `padH` contract). **Item 6 — the bar colours are the inverse of the theme.** Before: `surfaceColors()` "isle" ambient gave a bar button `bg = colorOpposite` / `fg = colorMain` (a panel is the reverse), and inverted to `bg = colorMain` when selected. Now the "isle" branch has **no resting fill and no border** — a bar button is a bare `colorOpposite` glyph/label sitting on the wallpaper, and only its selected (`active`) state paints a block, `bg = colorOpposite` / `fg = colorMain` — the exact same inversion a selected panel row uses, so the bar and a panel now share one colour language. `Config/Appearance.qml`: `barText` `colorMain`→`colorOpposite` (the centre-isle title is now on the wallpaper, not on an opposite block), `barButtonHover` is a 14%-alpha wash of `colorOpposite` (a solid mix reads wrong with no resting fill), `barIsleBackground` removed. `Widgets/WidgetStates.js` `surfaceColors()` splits into an explicit `ambient === "isle"` block ahead of the unchanged panel switch. Comment sync in `Widgets/Segment.qml` and `Bar/modules/Workspaces.qml`; the stale "(bar)" drops off the `radius-small` label in `Settings/sections/Theme.qml` (nothing on the bar uses that radius any more — the runner's name highlight and other small elements still do). **Not verified — no compositor here.** The real risk is legibility: `colorOpposite` text on whatever wallpaper is set (style plan constrains it to a wireframe grid or flat gradient, so it should be dark and low-contrast, but this is exactly a screenshot call). New/changed: `Widgets/BarIsle.qml`, `Widgets/WidgetStates.js`, `Config/Appearance.qml`, `Widgets/Segment.qml`, `Bar/modules/Workspaces.qml`, `Settings/sections/Theme.qml`. |

| OOP-24 | Shell restyle R4 — F: unified Alt+Tab / overview surface | verified | phi-shell ea61494 (+ 87852fd correction) · dotfiles this commit | 2026-09-10 | Batch F of `shell-restyle-r4` (see OOP-19), phi-shell only (the `hyprland.lua` bind/gesture cleanup is batch G). **Item 1 — unify SUPER+TAB and ALT+TAB (both broken) into one surface, ALT+TAB only.** `AltTab/AltTab.qml` rebuilt from the flat window strip into the user's layout: window boxes (icon over name, all `cellW × cellH`) grouped by workspace into **centred horizontal rows** — top row the lowest-numbered workspace, bottom row the highest — with the **full workspace list along the screen bottom**, centred, the workspace of the *selected* window highlighted (so Tab cycling moves the strip highlight coherently — `selectedWorkspaceId` is derived from the selected window, not the actually-focused workspace). Two open modes carried on `heldOpen`: **transient** (entered by the `ALT+Tab` Hyprland submap — `next`/`prev` cycle, `ALT` release → `confirm` → focus + close, `Escape` → `cancel`) and **persistent** (the three-finger-up gesture / a plain `toggle` — no key held, pointer-driven, closes on three-finger-down / a click on a box or pill / a click on the dim). A click on a window box runs `hyprctl dispatch focuswindow address:<addr>` (the mechanism `Launcher`'s `activateWindow` proves works — the wlr `Toplevel.activate()` was found not to focus) and closes; a click on a workspace pill runs `hyprctl dispatch workspace <id>` and closes. Window data is a **`hyprctl clients -j` snapshot on open** (the shape `Screenshot.qml` / the old confirm path / `phi`'s `internal/query/windows.go` all already parse — a momentary surface wants a snapshot, and it dodges betting on an unverified `Quickshell.Hyprland.HyprlandToplevel.workspace` field); the bottom strip reads the live `Services.HyprlandBridge.workspaces` (proven by `Bar/modules/Workspaces.qml`). The `IpcHandler` gains `open`/`close`/`toggle`; a **back-compat `overview` target** is kept, so the *current, unchanged* `hyprland.lua` (`Super+Tab` → `overview toggle`, the gestures → `overview open`/`close`) keeps working — OOP-24 is functional on its own, OOP-25 is cleanup. **Item 8 — the overview dim should match the notification panel / chat / cheatsheet and cover the status bar.** `AltTab` gets the OOP-16 treatment: `WlrLayer.Overlay` + `exclusiveZone: -1` + a `Widgets.Scrim` (it had none, `exclusiveZone: 0`, and stayed on the `Top` layer). `Overview/Overview.qml` is **retired** — left dormant in the tree (same as `Panels/tabs/Calendar.qml` after OOP-06), with a header note; `shell.qml` drops its instance + import. `Screenshot.qml`'s scrim also does not cover the bar but is deliberately left alone (a capture tool dimming the bar is questionable, and the user did not name it). **Not verified — no compositor here.** Screenshot-pass risks: vertical overflow with many workspace rows is not handled; a workspace with more windows than fit one row's width will clip (`Row`, no wrap); the `hyprctl clients -j` snapshot vs. `HyprlandToplevel` model choice; whether the transient surface on the `Overlay` layer with no `LayerFocus` ever mis-handles focus return on `cancel`; `Alt+Shift+Tab` as the *first* press lands on next-after-active, not previous (matches the old behaviour, more noticeable now). **Correction `87852fd` (§16.1):** the bottom workspace strip's pills were `Widgets.Segment` at the default `ambient: "panel"` (opaque `colorMain` squares on the scrim) — changed to `ambient: "isle"` so they match the bar's own workspace buttons after OOP-21 (bare digit, filled block only for the current). The window boxes stay `Widgets.Panel` (they are cards, not chrome) — a paired screenshot check with the strip since both sit on the dimmed surface. New/changed: `AltTab/AltTab.qml`, `Overview/Overview.qml` (header only), `shell.qml`. |

New `profiles/base/services-system.txt` **declares** it — the installer prints it and stops (R8); `sudo systemctl enable phi-vconsole-theme.service` is a user action. **(2) issue banner** — `profiles/base/system/etc/issue` reflowed: the Role-A ASCII Φ mark (unchanged, monochrome, `design/brand/phi-ascii.txt`) with the wordmark and `\s \r \m` / `\n \l` fields aligned into a tidy block beside it. **`I-05`**: `console.palette` holds literal 0-255 decimals traced to `PHI_ANSI_*` in the unit's header — `setvtrgb`'s format is bare decimals and the render pipeline only emits `#rrggbb`, so it cannot be templated; same known-divergence / manual-re-sync pattern as `design/brand/README.md`'s Φ assets, and `design/adapters.txt` gains a no-row note saying so. `kbd` (ships `setvtrgb`) is a hard dependency of `systemd`, not added. **Not verified — no machine here.** Unverified: whether `setvtrgb` on `/dev/tty0` repaints all VTs from a oneshot with no controlling terminal, the exact `setvtrgb` file-format edge cases, and the unit ordering. Revert on a running system: `setvtrgb vga`. | OOP-34 | `PHI_DOTFILES` resolution + manual-step completeness | awaiting-verification | phios-dotfiles `master` (this row's commit) | 2026-09-11 | **`Out-of-plan: auth-surfaces`** (the user's two closing asks: "many phi features don't work unless phios-dotfiles is in ~ — where is this specified and why", and "add all the manual steps to the manual.txt files"). **The `~` requirement:** `phi/internal/tokens/tokens.go` `Root()` resolves the checkout as `$PHI_DOTFILES`, else `~/phios-dotfiles`, else a hard error; `phi-shell/Config/Capabilities.qml` mirrors it (`${PHI_DOTFILES:-$HOME/phios-dotfiles}`). `phi` is pacman-installed and decoupled from any checkout, so it must be told or guess — S-12's row already flagged this as an undocumented gap. All three install procedures clone to `~/phios-dotfiles` (architettura §9.2.1 canonical), so a checkout kept anywhere else silently breaks `phi theme`, `phi doctor`'s dotfiles checks and the capability probe. **Fix (no `phi` rebuild):** new `bin/lib/env.sh` — `phios-install` now writes this checkout's real path to `~/.config/phios/dotfiles-root` and `~/.config/environment.d/10-phios.conf` on every apply (reported in `--dry-run`/`--check`, counts as one drift item until written); `profiles/base/home/.zshenv` reads the pointer file and exports `$PHI_DOTFILES` (canonical fallback when it is absent), so the TTY login shell and everything it starts — Hyprland, quickshell — inherit it. Both generated files are derived machine state, not manifest-tracked. `README.md` gains a "Where this checkout lives" section. A `phi/internal/tokens.Root()` fallback to the same pointer file (for non-shell contexts — cron, `ssh` non-interactive) is the recommended follow-up, held here to avoid reopening the `phi` build loop. **Manual steps:** `profiles/base/manual.txt` header broadened to cover applying `system/` material + enabling declared units; added the previously-undocumented S-53 banner install (`/etc/issue`, `/etc/phi-banner` + the sshd drop-in), S-45 theme-regen hook, and OOP-29's console palette + unit enable. New `profiles/desktop/manual.txt`: the S-34 `pam.d/phi-shell-lock` install (Lock.qml's own header flags its absence as fatal) and the S-53/OOP-33 Plymouth theme install + `plymouth-set-default-theme -R phi` + mkinitcpio-hook check. `laptop` (logind drop-in) and `workstation` (`crypttab`) still have no manual.txt — deferred, they belong to machine-install procedures, not post-install activation. **Verified here:** `bash -n` on the changed scripts, `zsh -n` on `.zshenv`, and `phios-install --dry-run --host razer` prints the new `environment` section and every new manual line. **Not verified — no machine here:** the environment.d propagation into a real session, and that a re-login actually makes `phi` find a non-`~` checkout. New: `bin/lib/env.sh`, `profiles/desktop/manual.txt`. Changed: `bin/phios-install`, `profiles/base/home/.zshenv`, `profiles/base/manual.txt`, `README.md`. |

Add some more if you have nice ideas." `phi-shell` only for code. **New `Config/LockPrefs.qml`** — a flat `lock.json` in `$XDG_STATE_HOME/phi` (`Paths.lockPrefsFile`), same mechanism/reasoning as `Config/ThemeOverrides` (not `phi state` — its key set is closed, `phi/internal/state/state.go` — so **no `phi` rebuild**). `effect` ∈ `none｜lava｜matrix｜starfield`, default **`lava`**. **New `Lock/LavaLamp.qml`** — Canvas soft-blob lava lamp (`lighter` compositing so blobs bloom; colour eases `accent`↔`info`); the headline effect. **New `Lock/Starfield.qml`** — calm parallax points, twinkling. `Lock/MatrixRain.qml` kept. **`Lock/Lock.qml`** — a `Loader` picks the effect from `Config.LockPrefs.effect` (`none` loads nothing); every effect exposes `running`, bound to freeze on the conceal fade. **`Settings/sections/Theme.qml`** — a "Lock screen effect" `StyledButton` row above Wallpaper, writing `Config.LockPrefs.setEffect()`; `Settings/sections.json` keywords updated. | OOP-37 | Settings overhaul — A: option index, highlight search, reveal, base widgets | awaiting-verification | phi-shell `main` · phios-dotfiles `master` (this row) | 2026-09-11 | **`Out-of-plan: settings-overhaul`** — the user asked to drastically improve the settings panel (style coherence in every section) and add features: a highlight-not-filter search that also drives system overlays, a much larger Theme section (colours + live `phi theme check`, fonts with previews, a cubic-bezier animation editor, wallpaper with images/modes/textures), Connectivity with WireGuard VPN + Tailscale + a `flow`-style Wi-Fi speed graph mirrored into the bar overlays, Devices with per-key Chroma + battery/notification/neovim integrations, grouped Keybindings shared with the Cheatsheet, per-app notification rules, and a rebuilt Update section (system state + one list per package manager). Branch `settings-overhaul` per repo (`phi-shell`, `phios-dotfiles`, `phi`); one commit + one OOP row per batch A–K; **not merged until the user confirms**; all VERIFY held to a combined handoff at the end (rounds like `shell-restyle` did). **Batch A, phi-shell:** `Settings/options.js` — the searchable catalogue of every option, id scheme `<sectionType>.<rest>` so a reveal derives the section with no lookup; `rank`/`score`/`matches`/`sectionMatches`/`topResult`. `Services/SettingsPanel.qml` gains `query`, `pendingReveal`, a row registry (`registerRow`/`unregisterRow`/`rowItem` + `rowRegistered` signal) and `reveal(id)`. `Settings/Settings.qml` search rewritten: the nav no longer filters — it highlights (new `highlighted` prop on `Widgets/ListRow.qml`, additive, default-off); Enter acts on `options.js` `topResult` (a section → `openSection`, an option → `reveal`); `_applyPendingReveal()` scrolls the content `Flickable` to the registered row and pulses it, retrying via `onRowRegistered` if the section is still loading; new IPC `settings.reveal(id: string)` / `settings.section(name: string)` (first typed-param `IpcHandler` methods in this shell — Quickshell documents them but no prior surface exercised it, flagged). New `Settings/sections/SettingsGroup.qml` (titled card over `Widgets/Panel`, the references/settings-reference.JPG shape) and `Settings/sections/SettingsRow.qml` (title + description + control slot + per-row reset + self-registration + query highlight + `pulse()`, category B — never a category-C scramble on a frequent event); both co-located with the section files so no cross-module import is needed. `Settings/sections/options.js` is the catalogue. New `Widgets/TextField.qml` (replaces the 3 inlined `TextInput` copies flagged in Theme/Keybindings/Devices), `Widgets/ColorPicker.qml` (pure-QtQuick HSV: SV square + hue strip + hex, no `Qt5Compat.GraphicalEffects`/shader) and `Widgets/ColorField.qml` (swatch + hex + inline-expand picker). **No section rewritten yet** — sections still use their current widgets, so a reveal to a specific option only selects the section until that section adopts `SettingsRow` (batches B–J). **Not verified — no compositor here:** the whole visual layer, the typed-param IPC, and `mapToItem`-based scroll targeting are the screenshot pass. |

Smoke-tested (every mode → valid PNG; identical bytes on identical inputs; bad mode errors); `go build`/`test`/`gofmt` clean here. **`phios-dotfiles`:** `design/tokens.common.sh` `PHI_TEXTURE_MODES` + `PHI_TEXTURE_INTENSITY_DEFAULT`; `Tokens.qml.tmpl` + phi-shell `docs/tokens-example.md` get the two rows. `phi/internal/wallpaper.Modes` must stay a superset of `PHI_TEXTURE_MODES` (noted in the token comment). **`phi-shell`:** `Background/Background.qml` → 3 composited layers (solid colour = also the window colour; tiled texture; image with per-mode `fillMode` + a `scale` transform for contain/repeat). `Services/Background.qml` holds all state, persists via `phi state`, lists the wallpaper folder (`ls` probe, the repo's own pattern), shells `phi wallpaper texture` once per (mode, intensity) into `$XDG_DATA_HOME/phi/textures`. `Settings/sections/Theme.qml` wallpaper group: solid-colour `ColorField`, thumbnail grid + none + add-from-path + open-folder (no native file picker — path field copies into the folder and selects; "Open folder" launches `xdg-open` for drag-drop), fit-mode buttons, scale `NumberField` (enabled only for image + contain/repeat), texture picker + intensity `Widgets/Meter` (enabled only where the colour shows through). **The style-plan "wireframe/gradient only, never photographic" warning is DELETED** — arbitrary images allowed (the user's explicit call). **S-44, the style plan, and I-01 now disagree with shipped behaviour** — needs a follow-up ADR recepiting "arbitrary wallpaper images allowed"; `docs/` untouched here, this is the flag. `Config/Tokens.qml` is `.gitignore`d and regenerated only by `phi theme set` — `Config/Appearance.qml` falls back to the known mode list until the user runs it. **Not verified — screenshot pass:** all compositing, texture legibility over a real colour, the `Image.Tile` + `scale` interaction, thumbnail loading. |

| OOP-42 | Settings overhaul — F: Connectivity + WireGuard VPN + Wi-Fi speed graph + overlays | awaiting-verification | phi `main` `c20b4fd` · phi-shell `main` `b6838bf` · phios-dotfiles `master` (this row) | 2026-09-11 | **`Out-of-plan: settings-overhaul`** batch F. **`phi`:** `internal/vpn` + `phi vpn list\|status [--json]\|up\|down`. (b) phi `902e254` — `phi vpn status` ran `sudo -n wg show` per up-tunnel every 15s poll; a missing sudoers drop-in meant a denied-sudo journal line every 15s. User chose **freeze-on-stop, centred**. `Magnifier/Magnifier.qml` rebuilt: lens centred ON the pointer, circular with a glass edge (plain Canvas — `createRadialGradient`, no effects module; true refraction = Q-F07, approximated by the rim shadow). A centred *live* `wlr-screencopy` feed is self-referential (the capture region is the lens's own hole), so the feed is **not live** — `ScreencopyView.captureFrame()` grabs a fresh still whenever the pointer settles, with the magnified layer hidden for the grab so the loupe never captures itself; slow keep-fresh recapture while parked; the last still is panned under the circle while moving. `SpringAnimation` on the lens position = the "liquid" trail/settle. `paintCursor:false` keeps the pointer out of the capture and the compositor draws the real cursor on top — no glyph of our own. **Retires OOP-50's offset lens.** Falls back to a live feed if `captureFrame()` is absent (logged). **Cannot be verified here:** recapture cadence, the hide/grab blink, `captureFrame` timing, `ScreencopyView` honouring the scaled size, `mask: Region {}` click-through — all for the screenshot pass. |

Glasscope-style circular loupe). **Merged to `main`/`master` on the user's instruction (2026-09-11)** — one

- **OOP-58** — built as **freeze-on-stop, centred** (the user's pick). A

  to exactly that. The still is regrabbed with the magnified layer hidden

  and the hide/grab timing being blink-free — screenshot pass decides.

### `converter-calc-magnifier` — status (2026-09-11)

Three existing runner/shell features made "very advanced" on one session
branch, requested out of plan. Branches (`phi`, `phi-shell`) merged into `main` with a `--no-ff`
commit each on the user's instruction (before any hardware pass);
`phios-dotfiles` landed as a single direct commit on `master` (no branch
was cut). Still **awaiting the hardware / screenshot verification**.

- **OOP-48** (`phi` `db1128e`): `internal/mathx` engine — advanced
  parser, ~20-dimension SI-expanded unit registry, symbolic derivatives,
  numeric integrals, equation/inequality solving with steps, plotting,
  probability. `Result.Rich` payload added (backward-compatible JSON).
- **OOP-49** (`phi-shell` `04aa787`): `Launcher/RichResult.qml` renders
  that payload as a card beside the runner box.
- **OOP-50** (`phi-shell` `0b4bb5b`, merge `f6c8346` · `master` `354c455`): `Magnifier/` loupe on
  `SUPER+Z`; `hyprland.lua.tmpl` bind swap.

**Apply path**

1. `git pull` `main` in `phi` and `phi-shell`, `master` in
   `phios-dotfiles` (the branches are merged).
2. Build + install `phi` from that branch (the runner shells out to the
   installed `phi query`).
3. `phi theme set <active variant>` — re-renders `hyprland.lua` with the
   new magnifier binds.
4. `pkill -x qs && qs -p ~/.config/quickshell/phi` (capture the log —
   `Services/Magnifier`, `Magnifier/Magnifier.qml`'s `ScreencopyView` and
   `mask: Region {}`, and `Launcher/RichResult.qml` are the most likely
   to need a one-line fix).
5. `hyprctl reload`.

**Verify**

- Runner: `100km to m`, `40% of 250`, `solve x^2-4=0`, `d/dx sin(x)`,
  `integrate x^2 from 0 to 3`, `plot sin(x)` — the card shows steps /
  roots / the plot; `100 usd to eur` still goes to the currency provider.
- `SUPER+Z` shows the loupe above the pointer; `SUPER`+wheel changes
  zoom, `SUPER+SHIFT`+wheel the lens size; Settings → Theme → Screen
  magnifier mirrors it. Screenshot the loupe and the runner card.

**Carried risks** — see each OOP row's Note: `ScreencopyView` sizing,
`mask` click-through, the offset-lens geometry, `mouse_up`/`mouse_down`
binds, the rectangular lens, and the rich-card overflow / narrow-width
stacking.

**Wallpaper — a shipped doc disagreement (needs a follow-up ADR)**

OOP-40 ships arbitrary wallpaper images (the user's explicit call, and the

as written. `docs/` was left untouched — this is the flag. A follow-up ADR
recording "arbitrary wallpaper images are allowed" is owed (same flag
OOP-40's own row raised).

**Apply path (whole track — do this in order)**

1. `git -C ~/phios-dotfiles checkout master && git -C ~/phios-dotfiles pull`
2. `git -C ~/phi checkout main && git -C ~/phi pull`
3. **Rebuild and install `phi`** from `main` (the usual chroot / `makepkg`
   flow, or `go build` and put it on `PATH`). **This is step one, not
   optional:** the shell calls `phi theme contrast`, `phi wallpaper texture`,
   `phi vpn status` and `phi pkg state/list` — without the new binary the
   Colours contrast badges read "checking…" forever, textures never
   generate, and the VPN / Updates groups are empty, and none of that looks
   like what it is.
4. `git -C ~/phi-shell checkout main && git -C ~/phi-shell pull`
5. **`phi theme set <active variant>`** — **required**: `Config/Tokens.qml`
   is git-ignored and only regenerated here, and the new
   `PHI_MOTION_B_BEZIER`, `PHI_TEXTURE_MODES` and
   `PHI_TEXTURE_INTENSITY_DEFAULT` tokens reach the shell only through it.
   Without it the Animations and Texture controls silently show
   `Config/Appearance.qml`'s built-in fallbacks instead of the tokens.
6. `pkill -x qs && qs -p ~/.config/quickshell/phi` — capture the full log.
   Most likely to need a one-line fix: the typed-param IPC handlers
   (`settings reveal`, `chroma nvimMode`), the `Services/Chroma.qml`
   `import qs.Services` self-import, `Pipewire.preferredDefaultAudioSink`,
   `easing.bezierCurve`, the `Widgets/Canvas` charts.
7. `(cp / install)` the nvim change is a plain file — `git pull` in dotfiles
   already placed `~/.config/nvim/lua/phi_chroma.lua`; restart nvim.
8. **WireGuard (optional, to test VPN):** `pacman -S wireguard-tools`; drop a
   `.conf` in `~/.config/phi/wireguard/`; to test up/down, install the
   drop-in: `sudo install -m 0440 ~/phios-dotfiles/profiles/desktop/system/etc/sudoers.d/49-phi-vpn /etc/sudoers.d/49-phi-vpn && sudo visudo -cf /etc/sudoers.d/49-phi-vpn`.

**Verification (paste back)**

```
# phi — on any host
(cd ~/phi && go test ./...)                              → all pass
phi pkg state                                            → phi + phios-dotfiles + phi-* versions
phi pkg state ; pacman -Q phi phi-shell                  → the `phi` line should agree with `pacman -Q phi` (a stale on-PATH binary is what step 3 guards against)
phi pkg list --manager appimage                          → ~/Applications/*.AppImage (or "none")
phi pkg list --manager npm                               → "not implemented" line
phi theme contrast '#d3a0ac' on '#1a1918'                → "7.85 pass"
phi wallpaper texture grain --intensity 40 --out /tmp/t.png && file /tmp/t.png   → PNG
phi vpn list                                             → tunnels in ~/.config/phi/wireguard (or nothing)
phi vpn status --json                                    → [] or a JSON array, no address field

# phi-shell — razer first, then zotac
qs -p ~/.config/quickshell/phi                           → starts, paste every QML warning/error
Super+S                                                  → panel opens
  search "accent"                                        → highlights (does NOT filter); Enter → Theme→Colours, pulsed
  search "wifi speed"  then Enter                        → Connectivity, pulsed on the speed graph
qs ipc call settings reveal devices.chroma.integrations  → panel opens on the Chroma integrations, pulsed

# Chroma — razer only. These settle every org.razer name in Services/Chroma.qml at once.
busctl --user call org.razer /org/razer razer.devices getDevices     → note the serial (first quoted string)
busctl --user introspect org.razer /org/razer/device/<serial>        → confirm setStatic / setKeyRow / setCustom names + signatures
busctl --user call org.razer /org/razer/device/<serial> razer.device.misc getMatrixDimensions   → the real matrix rows × cols
# in the panel: Devices → Chroma → toggle an integration, set a key colour
cat ~/.local/state/phi/chroma.json                       → the change is persisted

# nvim — razer
open nvim, switch modes                                  → keyboard tints (needs the "neovim" integration on + Chroma on)

screenshots: every settings section (General, Theme incl. Colours/Animations/Wallpaper,
  Connectivity, Devices, Keybindings, Notifications, Security, AI Agent, Updates),
  the cheatsheet, and the wifi / bluetooth / network bar overlays.
```

**Screenshot-pass checklist (known-fragile, not blockers)**

- `Updates.qml` / `AiAgent.qml`: five new `Repeater` delegates size off
  `parent.width` (the `SettingsGroup` `body` Column) — the same shape as the
  OOP-27 zero-width bug. If a list renders zero-width, that's the cause.
- `SettingsRow._first` (top-hairline suppression) misfires when the first
  child of a `SettingsGroup` body is a `Repeater` (Monitors, the Chroma
  integrations list, the per-app rules, the keybinding groups) — a spurious
  top hairline, cosmetic; the fix belongs in `SettingsRow` itself.
- `phi pkg state` shows only the `build` version of `phi`; if it disagrees
  with `pacman -Q phi` the mismatch is currently hidden (see the VERIFY
  line above).

**DONE WHEN** the user confirms each section is coherent with the system
style; the search highlights + reveals correctly (including from an
overlay's "Show in settings" button and via IPC); and the new features —
wallpaper compositing, editable motion, the WireGuard control, the Wi-Fi
graph, the Chroma per-key grid + all three integrations, the keybinding
grouping, the per-app rules, the Updates lists — behave on real hardware.

### `shell-restyle` — consolidated status (2026-09-10, verified 2026-09-11)

A single visual restyle of `phi-shell` + the design tokens, requested out of
plan, run over four feedback rounds. **Every row OOP-02 … OOP-25 is
`verified`** — the user ran the whole track on real hardware and confirmed
all four rounds in one pass on 2026-09-11 ("all verified, OOP-02…OOP-18 as
well"). One item remains open as a **deferred follow-up, not a defect**:
R4 item 5 (make the window-area gap equal the inter-window gap) — the repo
sets no Hyprland layout config and the declarative Lua call was not
verifiable from here; it waits on the user's `hyprctl getoption
general:gaps_{in,out}` / `~/.config/hypr` grep output (see OOP-25's row).

**Rounds**

- **R1 — OOP-02 … OOP-08** (`shell-restyle`): the two-colour B&W grammar,
  status bar rebuilt to isles, shared panels / cheatsheet / runner /
  notification panel / clipboard restyled, calendar placeholder, chat
  left-dock, centred Settings panel with an editable Theme section.
- **R2 — OOP-09 … OOP-14** (`shell-restyle-r2`): blank-Theme fix, wallpaper
  gap, notification-panel slide direction, cheatsheet spacing, `Φ` case,
  Settings close button (OOP-09); tighter 13px-base font scale, hairline
  1px panel border, 8px panel padding, accent off titles (OOP-10); btop
  quoting bug + `icon+value` bar indicators + click-popouts + full-height
  buttons (OOP-11); runner centred / fixed-height / apps-by-default /
  coherent sizes / wider (OOP-12); cheatsheet columns + Settings keyword
  search (OOP-13); expanded Neovim theme + English template headers
  (OOP-14).
- **R3 — OOP-15 … OOP-18** (`shell-restyle-r3`, 11 items; 8 landed):
  - OOP-15 (A): #3 tighter bar height/margins, #4 runner padding + no
    input/options rule, #8 centre-isle horizontal padding.
  - OOP-16 (B): #1 modal panels raised to `WlrLayer.Overlay` +
    `exclusiveZone: -1` so the scrim dims the bar; notification panel got a
    scrim.
  - OOP-17 (C): #2 bar popouts drop below their button (`Segment.centerX()`);
    #9 volume/brightness popout is a draggable `overlay-reference` pill via
    the new `Widgets/Meter.qml` (also adopted by `Osd/Osd.qml`);
    wifi/bluetooth/network/battery popouts carry a readout + TUI deep-link.
  - OOP-18 (D): the active-window centre isle is now exactly the side-isle
    height (`BarIsle` gains `padH` separate from `pad`; bar height no longer
    keys off the centre isle, so it doesn't resize when an app
    opens/closes); isle Segments halved their vertical inset.
- **R4 — OOP-19 … OOP-25** (`shell-restyle-r4`, 10 items):
  - OOP-19 (A): item 9 selected list rows / panels invert their text
    (`ListRow` + `Panel.contentColor`); item 7 runner name↔directory pushed
    to opposite ends.
  - OOP-20 (B): item 2 — one `Services/BarMetrics` source for bar height;
    the notification / chat docks start below the bar (scrim still covers it).
  - OOP-21 (C): items 6 + 10 — the isle background is gone; a bar button is
    a bare opposite-coloured glyph on the wallpaper, boxed only when
    selected (was the inverse of a panel). **This closes R3 #7**, held then.
  - OOP-22 (D): item 4 — bar popouts align their right edge to the button's
    right edge, one rhythm unit below the bar (`Segment.rightX()`).
  - OOP-23 (E): item 3 — the volume/brightness function keys get the OSD

  - OOP-24 (F): item 1 — one unified Alt+Tab surface (window boxes grouped

    Overview retired. Item 8 — it gets the OOP-16 scrim treatment.
  - OOP-25 (G): item 1 compositor side — `Super+Tab` bind removed, the

    gaps) is HELD** — no Hyprland layout config exists in this repo and the

**Apply path (whole track)**

1. `git pull` in `phios-dotfiles` (through OOP-25's commit).
2. `phi theme set <active variant>` — **required**: OOP-10's 1px border /

**R3 items NOT built — pending this verification + a disk fix**

- **#5 + #10** — kitty window chrome (padding, border) and the yazi /
  Librewolf theme files. yazi's `theme.toml` needs a proper expansion but
  yazi rejects unknown keys and falls back to its default theme, so the
  user's yazi version is needed first. Librewolf's `userChrome.css` is two
  lines and has no `adapters.txt` row (random profile-dir prefix — see that
  template's header).
- **#6 — done at OOP-28, revised at OOP-31 (out-of-plan, `auth-surfaces`).**
  `Lock/Lock.qml`: terminal-style password field (mono role, a
  `cursorDelegate` block caret blinking on `PHI_MOTION_A_PERIOD`, `*` echo,
  `radius-small`); a real fade in/out (OOP-31 — explicit `NumberAnimation`
  at the category-C duration, deferred via `Qt.callLater`; conceal on
  unlock is now the sole writer of `locked = false`); a full-surface
  `Qt.BlankCursor` `MouseArea` (mouse + touch); and a `Canvas` matrix-rain
  backdrop (`Lock/MatrixRain.qml`). Awaiting the screenshot pass — see
  OOP-28 / OOP-31's rows.
- **#7 — CLOSED at OOP-21 (R4).** The user confirmed it means the per-isle
  blocks, and added the colour-inversion half (R4 item 6). `BarIsle` no
  longer paints a block; `surfaceColors()`'s "isle" branch has no resting
  fill or border; `barText`/`barButtonHover` re-derived. Legibility over
  the wallpaper is a screenshot-pass check.
- **#11** — btop / Steam workspace buttons. btop button = switch to the
  `special:btop` workspace, launch btop if absent, unify with the existing
  special-workspace feature. A Steam button appears after the numbered
  workspaces (before btop) whenever a Steam window exists, switching to
  `name:steam`. Planned shape: a workspace-existence check off
  `Hyprland.workspaces` (`name:steam` appears once a window lands there via
  the `hyprland.lua` class rule) — no new service surface.

**Known unverifiable risks carried into the screenshot pass**

- `Bar/glyphs.js` — 17 Nerd-Font `nf-md` codepoints, never checked against
  the font. All-boxes = `font-symbol` not resolving; some-boxes = specific
  wrong codepoints. Value text always renders regardless.
- `Quickshell.DesktopEntries.applications` — guarded; worst case the
  runner's empty-query browse list is empty (typed queries still work).
- `Font.DemiBold` on `Source Sans 3` — if the face is absent, titles won't
  look distinct from body text (they lost their accent colour in OOP-10).
- `btop` special-workspace `active` state is a local bool (no compositor
  property to read); the `hyprctl clients -j | grep '"class": "phios-btop"'`
  match depends on hyprctl's exact JSON spacing.
- Exact px for the size scale (`11/13/14/16/18/21/24`) and panel padding
  (8px) are judgment calls — index 1 (13px) also drives every `1ch` gap.
- **R4:** bar glyphs/text as `colorOpposite` directly on the wallpaper
  (OOP-21) — depends entirely on which wallpaper is set. The unified
  `AltTab.qml` (OOP-24) has never rendered: `hyprctl clients -j` snapshot
  shape, `DesktopEntries.heuristicLookup` icon hit-rate, no handling yet
  for vertical overflow (many workspace rows) or a workspace with more
  windows than fit one row, and whether the transient (Alt-held) surface
  on the Overlay layer ever fumbles focus return on `cancel`. The OSD pill
  height is a rough formula. `Services/SettingsPanel`'s section-jump
  (`openSection`) assumes `registryRows` is loaded.

**Environment note (2026-09-10):** the workstation disk hit 0 bytes free
mid-session (APFS local snapshots the likely cause); `df -h /` recovered to
~1.3 GB after cleanup. `phi theme set` and `git pull` write to that disk —
free space before applying.

### `shell-features` — out of plan (2026-09-10, merged to main/master 2026-09-11)

A batch of five feature requests, out of plan, one commit per repo per
feature, then merged. **Nothing here has run on real hardware.**

**Merge (2026-09-11).** `github/main` (phi, phi-shell) and `github/master`
(dotfiles) had all moved on to the `converter-calc-magnifier` track
(OOP-48..50) since the branch was cut. All three merges were textually
clean; one semantic collision resolved:
- `hyprland.lua.tmpl`: SF-1 had bound `Super`+mouse-wheel to workspace
  scroll; OOP-50's magnifier binds the same combo to loupe zoom (and would
  win the collision anyway — last bind for a key combo wins). SF-1's
  wheel binds were **dropped** (workspace switching stays on `Super`+1..0
  and `Super`+arrows); the `Super`+`mouse:272/273` drag/resize binds are
  unaffected (mouse buttons, not the wheel).
- `phi-shell/Settings/sections/options.js`: added the `theme.magnifier`
  catalogue entry OOP-50 forgot (it registers that `optionId` but never
  listed it, so it logged a warning).
- **Known, NOT fixed here** — OOP-50's `Services/Magnifier.qml` writes
  `magnifier.zoom` / `magnifier.size` through `phi state`, but neither key
  is in `phi/internal/state/state.go`'s closed `Keys` set, so those writes
  are rejected and the magnifier's zoom/size do not persist. That is a
  `converter-calc-magnifier` gap, already on `main` before this merge —
  flagged, left for that track's owner.

| # | Feature | Repos | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| SF-1 | Keybindings + click-to-focus | dotfiles (`hyprland.lua.tmpl`), phi-shell (`Services/Keybinds.qml`) | awaiting-verification (Super+wheel workspace-scroll dropped at merge — magnifier owns it) |
| SF-2 | WireGuard: custom-conf detection + import/manage | phi (`internal/vpn`, `internal/cli`), phi-shell (`Services/Vpn`, `Settings/sections/Connectivity`, `Panels/BarPopout`, `Config/Paths`), dotfiles (`profiles/desktop/manual.txt`) | awaiting-verification |
| SF-3 | btop / Steam special-workspace rebuild | phi-shell (bar), dotfiles (`hyprland.lua.tmpl` comments only) | **superseded by BF-5 / ADR 134** — the special-workspace model it built is removed; only its `Bar/glyphs.js` `steam` glyph survives |
| SF-4 | Notifications: sound, test button, clean, groups, retention, bar blink | phi-shell + dotfiles (`profiles/desktop/packages.txt`) | awaiting-verification |
| SF-5 | Cursor spotlight: effect picker, optimisation, top z-index | phi-shell | awaiting-verification |

`sound-theme-freedesktop` was added to `profiles/desktop/packages.txt` by
the user's explicit choice (extra/T0, not in master plan §15) — it provides
the named default notification sounds. `pacman -S sound-theme-freedesktop`
on `zotac`/`razer`.

**SF-1 (keybindings + click-to-focus).** `hyprland.lua.tmpl`:
- `hl.config({ input = { follow_mouse = 0 } })` — first `hl.config` call in
  the file; hover no longer focuses, only a click does. A config variable,
  so it survives `hyprctl reload` (a `hyprctl keyword` in an
  `hyprland.start` hook would not — that event does not refire on reload).
- Window management binds that never existed: move window (`Super+Shift+`
  arrows), numbered workspaces + send-to-workspace (`Super+1..0` /
  `Super+Shift+1..0`, the `i % 10` loop verbatim from `example/hyprland.lua`),
  `Super+mouse:272/273` drag/resize, fullscreen (`Super+F`), float toggle
  (`Super+Shift+F`), dwindle split (`Super+J`), centre (`Super+C`), pin
  (`Super+Shift+P`), a `special:scratch` scratchpad (`Super+A` /
  `Super+Shift+A`), monitor focus/move (`Super+,` / `Super+.` — inert on a
  single output), and a `Super+R` resize submap. (A `Super+wheel` workspace
  scroll was in the branch but dropped at merge — see the merge note above.)
- API forms: `hl.dsp.focus({ workspace = N })`, `hl.dsp.window.move({
  workspace = N })`, `hl.dsp.window.float`, `hl.dsp.layout("togglesplit")`,
  `hl.dsp.workspace.toggle_special`, the mouse binds and the scroll pair are
  all verbatim from `hyprwm/Hyprland`'s `example/hyprland.lua` (fetched this
  session) — used natively. Everything the example did NOT show (direction
  moves, centre, pin, per-key resize, monitor focus/move, fullscreen) goes
  through `hl.dsp.exec_cmd("hyprctl dispatch …")` deliberately: a wrong
  `hl.dsp.*` shape can abort the whole Lua script on load, and `hyprctl
  dispatch` is stable. They can move to native forms once confirmed on
  hardware. C-06 (Lua API in motion) applies.
- `Services/Keybinds.qml` `context()` classifier updated so the new binds
  land in "Window management" in the settings/cheatsheet reference rather
  than "Other".
- Reaches machines with `git pull` + `phi theme set <variant>` (re-renders
  the template) + `hyprctl reload`. No `phi` rebuild.

**SF-2 (WireGuard custom-conf + import/manage).** The user's tunnel was in
`/etc/wireguard` (root-only) and up already, so `phi vpn` — which only
scanned `~/.config/phi/wireguard` — showed nothing.
- `phi/internal/vpn`: `List()`/`Status()` now union three sources — the
  managed dir, `/etc/wireguard` (best-effort `os.ReadDir`, silent on the
  usual permission denial), and `ip link show type wireguard` (unprivileged,
  so it never trips the `sudo -n wg show` detail backoff). `TunnelStatus`
  gains `Managed` + `Origin` ("managed" | "etc" | "external"). New
  `Import(src, name)` (validates `[Interface]`, copies 0600 into the managed
  dir, refuses a dup) and `Forget(name)` (managed dir only — refuses an
  `/etc` or interface-only tunnel). `Up()` uses the full managed path when
  it has one, else the bare name for wg-quick to resolve against
  `/etc/wireguard`.
- `phi/internal/cli/vpn`: `import PATH [NAME]` and `forget NAME` verbs;
  `status` plain output gains the origin column; `--json` gains the two
  fields.
- phi-shell: `Services/Vpn` parses the new fields and adds
  `importConfig(path)` / `forget(name)`. `Settings/sections/Connectivity`'s
  VPN group is always shown with disabled placeholder controls when empty
  (not a collapsed prose line), a per-tunnel origin readout, a Forget button
  for managed tunnels, an Import-for-`/etc`-tunnels button, and an
  import-a-path row + "Open folder". `Panels/BarPopout` shows a disabled
  "no tunnels" toggle instead of a bare line. `Config/Paths` gains
  `vpnConfigDir`.
- dotfiles: `profiles/desktop/manual.txt` finally carries the
  `sudoers.d/49-phi-vpn` install + `visudo -c` lines (they only ever
  existed as a comment inside the sudoers file itself).
- Reaches machines: dotfiles side is `git pull` only (manual.txt). The

- Verified off-machine (macOS, native build): import / list / status

  Linux host); `go build` / `vet` / `gofmt` clean.

**SF-3 (btop / Steam special-workspace rebuild).** `Bar/modules/Btop.qml`
deleted; new `Bar/modules/SpecialWorkspaces.qml` (type `specialWorkspaces`,
`modules.json` position 15 — right after `workspaces`, so its buttons
always sit to the right of the numbered strip). Driven by the new
`Bar/pinned-apps.json` (ADR 078):

**SF-4 (notifications: sound, test, clean, groups, retention, bar blink).**
All phi-shell.
- `Services/Notifications`: new prefs (`retentionDays` = 7, `soundEnabled`

**SF-5 (cursor spotlight: effect picker, optimisation, top z-index).** All
phi-shell.
- OPTIMISATION. `Spotlight/Spotlight.qml` no longer repaints a full-screen
  Canvas on every cursor poll. The dim/flashlight vignette is a small Canvas
  "sprite" (radial gradient, transparent → solid scrim) painted once and
  re-painted only on an option change; the rest of the screen is four plain
  scrim `Rectangle`s that resize to tile around the sprite square. Cursor
  movement now only moves GPU-composited items — no CPU repaint. The
  `hyprctl cursorpos` poll stays (no cursor-move event exists), bumped 60→55
  ms.
- EFFECTS. `Services/Spotlight` owns `effect` (dim | flashlight | crosshair
  | ring) + per-effect options (size, dim strength; line thickness/opacity;
